# v2 backend deviations log

Append-only. One dated row for every place where the API differs from what the mock, the contract or a doc said. A row without an agreement in "Who agreed" is not valid.

| Date | Item | Mock / contract / doc said | API does | Why | Who agreed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 `POST /auth/password/email`, unknown e-mail | Mock: 422 "We can't find a user with that email address." | 200 "We have emailed your password reset link." for a known and an unknown e-mail alike; same work and timing | No account enumeration (card AC3) | User D55b (2026-10-19) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 `POST /auth/password/reset`, unknown e-mail | Mock: 422 "We can't find a user with that email address." on `fields.email` | 422 "This password reset token is invalid." on `fields.email` (same as a bad token) | No account enumeration; beyond card AC3 | User D55b (2026-10-19) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 `POST /auth/password/reset-ic` attempt limit | Mock: no limit | 429 RATE_LIMITED (BM message, `retryAfter`, `Retry-After`) after 5 attempts per 15 minutes per account and per IP; every attempt counts | Online guessing of the IC number takes over accounts of minors | User D55a (2026-10-19) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 `POST /auth/register` and `POST /auth/password/reset`: response body | Mock: `{ token, expiresAt, user }` (register), `{ token, expiresAt, user }` + status (reset) | Contract `Session` (`{ data: Session }`, reset adds the sibling `status`) with the session cookie and `Cache-Control: no-store`; no token in any body | UD-1: the contract wins over the mock for session and ids | UD-1 (2026-10-19) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 `GET /me`, `PUT /me`: `AccountUserMe` | Mock `UserMe`: single `role`, no `username` | `role` is the ACTIVE role; adds `roles` (all roles) and `username` so it agrees with `GET /session` (AC8) | Session users may have no e-mail; one source of truth for roles | Pending LEAD (card open question 1) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 `POST /me/notifications/bulk-read` and `bulk-delete`: `ids` | Mock: integer ids, message "ids.* must be an integer" | `ids` are `Id` strings; an unknown or foreign id is ignored like a foreign one; no "must be an integer" message | UD-1: opaque string ids | UD-1 (2026-10-19) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 `POST /auth/password/reset-ic` response and token rules | Mock: `{ token, email }` where the token is the reset row id | `data.token` only (the e-mail is not echoed); token is at least 128-bit random, only its hash stored, 60 min, single use, a new issuance revokes earlier tokens, a successful reset consumes all of the user's tokens, constant-time compare; `Cache-Control: no-store`; no token for an inactive account; audit event and in-app notice on issue and on reset | Security review 2026-10-19 (High: IC knowledge equals account control); D57b | LEAD (D57, delegated by the user 2026-10-19) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 per-e-mail throttle of the reset flow | Mock: 60 s per e-mail only for known e-mails; reset-ic started it only on a match | Throttle keyed on the hash of the normalised e-mail for every `password/email` request and every reset-ic attempt, known e-mail or not; plus per-(e-mail hash, IP) and per-IP limits (BE-03 numbers: 5 failures per 60 s, per-IP ceiling 20 proposed) on `password/reset`, each with 429 `Retry-After` | A 429 must never reveal a correct IC guess or an existing e-mail; no targeted lock-out of a victim from another IP | LEAD (D57a) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 reset link and logs | Mock: link `/password/reset/<token>?email=<email>`, dev mail logged in full | Token (and e-mail) in the URL fragment, `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` on the reset pages; the dev MailPort logs only a masked e-mail and a token id; IC, token and e-mail redacted from logs and error bodies | BE-NFR-10, BE-NFR-11 | LEAD (D57) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-06 429 on write routes and `GET /public/stats` | Mock: no throttle | Every write route (account and notifications) and the public lookup documents 429 RATE_LIMITED with `retryAfter` and `Retry-After` | D57a | LEAD (D57a) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 429 RATE_LIMITED | Mock has no rate limit on any M27 / Amaran route. | Every `/admin-sekolah` write, both reset-password routes, both user `export.csv` routes and the Amaran CSV answer 429 `RATE_LIMITED` with `retryAfter` and the `Retry-After` header. Limits are set in the implementation card and the runbook. | Bulk account minting, e-mail enumeration and bulk dumps of minors' data (D57a, BE-NFR-04). | D57 (LEAD, delegated by the user); LEAD review BE-21 |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 no-store | Mock sets no cache headers except the Amaran CSV. | `Cache-Control: no-store` on the 201 of `POST /admin-sekolah/pelajar` (plaintext one-time password) and on both user CSV exports. | A cache must not keep a minor's password or a list of minors (D57b). | D57; LEAD review BE-21 |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 lost 201 / Idempotency-Key | Mock returns the credential once; a retry gives 422 "The email has already been taken." and nothing says how to recover. | Documented: the credential is never re-served; recovery is `POST /admin-sekolah/pelajar/{id}/reset-password`. Optional `Idempotency-Key` header on the create; a replayed key answers 409 CONFLICT (proposed) and never creates a second account. The plaintext is never stored for a retry. | Safe retry without keeping a plaintext secret (BE-NFR-18). The header and the 409 are proposed. | LEAD review BE-21 (recovery rule); Idempotency-Key + 409 proposed, pending LEAD |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 e-mail enumeration | Create and update return 422 "The email has already been taken." for an address used in ANY school. | Kept as is, recorded as an accepted trade-off; mitigated by 429 and the audit trail. | Unique e-mails cannot be hidden without dropping uniqueness. | LEAD review BE-21 ("record as known and accepted") |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 Admin (Pusat) excluded | Card AC1 says "restricted to school_admin (and admin)". | `/admin-sekolah/*` is school_admin only; Admin gets 403 (no school to scope to; a missing school never means all schools). Admin keeps the `/amaran/*` routes, which carry an explicit school id. | The mock handlers already refuse every role except school_admin. The card AC1 must be amended. | LEAD review BE-21; card amendment needs the LEAD |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 self-toggle | Card AC3: toggle-active cannot disable the caller's own account. The mock has no refusal (teachers only; the caller is not a teacher, so a self-target is 404). | Contract states: a self-target answers 404 "Not Found." today; if the managed roles widen, 422 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `fields.id` "Anda tidak boleh menyahaktifkan akaun sendiri." | One agreed behaviour for backend and frontend. The BM copy is proposed (modelled on M02 `MSG_OWN_DELETE`). | LEAD review BE-21; copy proposed, pending LEAD |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 password length | Mock UI: create teacher, update and reset-password say "Minimum 6 aksara" and accept 6; create pupil and the form options use 8. | Every password field is 8 to 255 characters (create pupil, create teacher, update `newPassword`, both reset-password routes; confirmations at most 255). The frontend placeholders "Minimum 6 aksara" (`CiptaGuruPage.vue`, `SchoolUserEditForm.vue`) must become 8. | One policy; 6 characters on a minor's account is too weak; the 255 cap keeps oversized input away from the hasher. | LEAD review BE-21 |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 Amaran out-of-scope school | Mock: a school outside the caller scope is 403; the CSV route checks 404 before access. | `GET /amaran/schools/{id}`, `/amaran/summary` (school scope) and `/amaran/export/school/{id}`: unknown and out-of-scope school are both 404 "Not Found."; 403 only for a disallowed role (and the district / state scope of a non-admin). The CSV does the role check (403) before the 404. | BE-NFR-05: 403 versus 404 would let a caller enumerate valid school ids. | LEAD review BE-21 |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 mass assignment | BE-NFR-05 wants a strict schema that rejects unknown fields with 422. The mock ignores `role`, `schoolId`, `isActive`, `grade`, `icNumber` and other keys. | Waiver recorded: unknown and protected keys are ignored, never bound and never an error. The implementation must prove it (a body with `role=admin` creates a student; a `classId` of another school is 422 and never enrols). | Mock fidelity; the frontend sends no such keys anyway. | LEAD review BE-21 ("acceptable, the card must note the waiver") |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 default password | Empty `password` = the configured shared default password, echoed once in the 201. | Kept, and the account is flagged `mustChangePassword: true` (D55c / D57c). Decided D59b (superseded by the 2026-10-19 row "BE-21 initial password" below). Alternative: the server generates a random initial password per account and returns it in the same `credential.password` field; the wire shape does not change. | A shared predictable default is a risk for minors; the flag limits the exposure to the first login. | Decided D59b (LEAD, relayed by the user) |
| 2026-10-19 | BE-21 no-store + Pragma (round 2) | Round 1 declared `Cache-Control: no-store` on the pupil 201 and the user CSVs only. | `Cache-Control: no-store` and `Pragma: no-cache` are declared on the pupil 201, both user CSVs, the Amaran CSV and both reset-password 200 responses (the request body carries a password). Tested. | D57b; LEAD review BE-21 round 2. | D57; LEAD review BE-21 round 2 |

| 2026-10-20 | BE-36 follow-up: errors, timestamps and status codes vs the prototype | Prototype: a failure is a thrown `Error` and the upload screen prints `e.message` or "Pengekstrakan gagal." under the title "Pengekstrakan gagal"; a real provider without server configuration throws "Dikonfigurasi di pelayan"; an unknown provider id throws "Penyedia AI tidak dikenali: ${id}" at start-up; batch times are epoch numbers (`createdAt: number`); the prototype has no AI status, usage, settings or test call | Failures use the contract error format `{ error: { code, message } }` with the D54/D57 codes: `AI_UNAVAILABLE` 503 (carrying "Dikonfigurasi di pelayan" when the provider is not configured, the [PROPOSED] unreachable sentence otherwise), `AI_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED` 503, `RATE_LIMITED` 429 with `retryAfter` and `Retry-After` (D57a), 403 for a non-admin role or a missing CSRF token, 401 without a session. "Pengekstrakan gagal." is the `message` of a failed run whose cause is not an `Error` (`AI_MESSAGES.extractionFailed`). The unknown-provider sentence stays a start-up error and never travels over HTTP. `updatedAt`, `createdAt` and `checkedAt` are the contract `Timestamp` (ISO 8601 with the +08:00 offset), never epoch numbers. Provider ids and labels equal the prototype registry (`AI_PROVIDER_LABELS`); the `/ai/status`, `/ai/usage`, `/ai/settings` and `/ai/test` shapes, `AiFailure`, usage, redaction, `generateText` and `suggestTags` have no prototype source and stay [PROPOSED] | The wire needs machine codes and the one error format of the API (D54, D57); the prototype can only show a message. Copy that exists in the prototype is used verbatim | Pending LEAD (BE-36 follow-up review) |

| 2026-10-20 | BE-08 cascade delete (follow-up; supersedes the "cascade delete: open question, no change" row and narrows the "cascade deletes and later question FKs" row for the admin API) | Card AC4 and the mock: deleting a level or subject deleted its chapters, topics and subtopics | Every delete route answers 409 `CONFLICT` and deletes nothing while the entity has children. Level: linked subjects, chapters (in the tree or not) or DSKP nodes, "Tahap Pendidikan ini masih mempunyai subjek, bab atau nod DSKP." Subject: level links, chapters, DSKP nodes or TP descriptors, "Subjek ini masih mempunyai tahap, bab atau nod DSKP." Chapter: topics, "Bab ini masih mempunyai topik." Topic: subtopics, "Topik ini masih mempunyai subtopik." A subtopic has no children and deletes as before. The check runs in the delete transaction after `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` on the parent row; a concurrent child insert takes a key-share lock on that row, so it either commits first (the delete then sees it and answers 409) or waits and fails its foreign key (422 on the referencing field, like any vanished parent). The 404 check and copy come first. The DB FKs stay `ON DELETE CASCADE` (migration 0008 untouched, no new migration). Chapter and topic delete responses keep their shape with `topics` and `subtopics` always 0. Contract: 409 added to the four delete routes, `CURRICULUM_CONFLICT_MESSAGES` exported | The LEAD wanted a branch of the curriculum never erased in two clicks. DSKP nodes and TP descriptors are included because they also cascade silently and have no admin route; the consequence is that the seeded DSKP-only subjects LNR and KNP (nodes, no links, no chapters) cannot be deleted through the API. The four BM sentences are [PROPOSED] (the LEAD example wording extended with the DSKP nodes; no observed copy exists) | LEAD (2026-10-20); copy is a working assumption (BE-08 follow-up) |

Reads (admin and learner) are not limited. Contract: 429 (with the `Retry-After` header) added to the 17 write routes. Schemes http and https, mailto for links only, no `data:`, no `javascript:`, no protocol-relative; `<a>` always gets `rel="noopener noreferrer"` and only root-relative or `#` relative links survive; `<img src>` survives ONLY as `/api/files/<22-character token>` (relative, or on a `PUBLIC_ORIGINS` origin without query or fragment), so third-party pixels and other `/api` GETs are dropped; script, style, iframe, object, svg, math and similar are dropped with their content; other unknown tags are discarded and their text kept (the fallback prints them as text); NUL is stripped first. Output differences from the fallback, all inert: void elements print as `<br />`, entity references print as the character, nesting is capped at 100 levels by `nestingLimit` (the fallback caps at 64; output is always balanced) | Security review LOW-1; library over hand-rolled parsing | LEAD (2026-10-20) |

| 2026-10-20 | BE-37 PDF worker memory watchdog | Card: bounded memory for untrusted PDFs (worker `resourceLimits`) | Besides `maxOldGenerationSizeMb = OCR_WORKER_MEMORY_MB`, the engine watches the process RSS while a worker runs and terminates it (`TOO_COMPLEX`) when RSS grows by more than twice `OCR_WORKER_MEMORY_MB` | `resourceLimits` do not cover ArrayBuffers and native memory of a worker, which is where a decompression bomb lives; a polling watchdog is the only parent-side bound | BE-37 design brief (Delivery Lead, 2026-10-20) |

No path or schema changed; `openapi.json` re-emitted (tags only) | BE-37 design brief (Delivery Lead, 2026-10-20) |
| 2026-10-20 | BE-37 active-batch limit message | Contract: over `INTAKE_MAX_ACTIVE_BATCHES` is 429 RATE_LIMITED | The 429 uses the existing `INTAKE_FILE_MESSAGES.quota` text ("Had kuota muat naik harian telah dicapai. Cuba lagi esok."), with `retryAfter` 3600 | DESIGN adds no separate copy for this limit; the text is slightly off for a limit that frees when a batch finishes or is cancelled. LEAD may approve a dedicated line | BE-37 design brief (Delivery Lead, 2026-10-20) |
| 2026-10-20 | BE-37 content bombs are caught by the page job, not the upload | Card AC3: a zip/PDF bomb sample is rejected with a 4xx | A ZIP or any non-PDF is 422 (type). A PDF whose page tree is small but whose content stream inflates hugely passes `inspect` (it reads the page count only); reading the page in the worker hits the time/memory limit, the page fails `TOO_COMPLEX`/`OCR_TIMEOUT`, the file ends `failed` and the API and worker stay up. A per-file circuit breaker limits the cost: after two pages of a file fail with TOO_COMPLEX, OCR_TIMEOUT or RENDER_FAILED the file is failed (`TOO_COMPLEX`), its other pages fail with it and its queued and running jobs are cancelled | Rejecting such a file at upload would mean decoding every page inside the request; the sandboxed page job gives the same safety without the cost. LEAD to confirm | BE-37 design brief (Delivery Lead, 2026-10-20) |
| 2026-10-20 | BE-37 job terminal-failure hook | Job runner design: a job that fails for good only changes its own row | `register(type, handler, { onExhausted })` is called once when a job ends `failed` (out of attempts, non-retryable error, or swept as `STUCK`) with the final code; best effort, errors logged. Intake uses it to fail the page or file and close the batch | Without it a page whose retries ran out stayed `pending` and the batch stayed `processing` for ever | BE-37 review and security findings (Delivery Lead, 2026-10-20) |
| 2026-10-20 | BE-37 claim fairness and per-claim tokens | Design: claim = `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` of the oldest due jobs, `locked_by` = worker id | Jobs are claimed one per statement and a queued job is skipped while another job of the same `entity_type` + `entity_id` (one file) is running, so pages of one file run one at a time; `locked_by` is `<worker>:<uuid>` per claim, and complete/fail/cancel-check use it | One large or hostile file could otherwise occupy every slot; with a worker-wide id a run that outlived a sweep could close the same job after a later claim by the same process | BE-37 review and security findings (Delivery Lead, 2026-10-20) |
| 2026-10-20 | BE-37 upload concurrency and PDF inspect pool | Design: sync validation in the request, bounded only by `OCR_WORKERS` | `INTAKE_UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY` (default 2) uploads run at once in a process, the rest wait (not fail); files are inspected one after the other and the first refusal stops the request; a client that disconnects aborts the inspect; the PDF engine has a separate slot pool for inspect so page jobs cannot starve uploads | Bound CPU and memory spent on untrusted PDFs before authentication-cost abuse becomes possible | BE-37 review and security findings (Delivery Lead, 2026-10-20) |

Two keys in production: `--force-demo` is accepted only together with `ALLOW_DEMO_SEED=true` (or `1`); the flag alone is refused with a clear error and a non-zero exit before any database work, loading nothing (not even the reference modules). `ALLOW_DEMO_SEED=true` alone works as before, and outside production the flag behaves as before. Production is what the seed guard (`assertSeedAllowed`) refuses. `pocProfileSeed.run` itself still throws in production without an opt-in (`assertSeedAllowed`), so the demo data can never be written by accident. Pinned by `seed-production.spec.ts` and `poc-guard.spec.ts` (flag only refused, env only allowed, both allowed, non-production unchanged) | The production image runs `db:seed` on deploy to load the reference data (BE-25 reference-only contract; e6f52c6d ships the DSKP JSON so `db:seed` runs inside the image). A non-zero exit when no flag is given would fail every production deploy, and passing `--force-demo` there to avoid it would publish the demo accounts. The safe property AC7 protects (no demo data in production without an explicit opt-in) holds, and the second key (Minor 4) stops a stray flag alone from loading demo data | Accepted by LEAD 2026-10-21 (#67), with the two-key rule |

| 2026-10-21 | AZ-20 Bijak admin intake review over http (frontend wiring of BE-37/38/09) | Mock: a batch is one document (`saveExtracted`), "Hantar untuk semakan" moves it to Semakan and enqueues review units that "Lulus & Terbit" publishes; the bank is Bijak-typed with versions ("Sunting" = version n+1), kertas and topic; the unit list of the editor is the prototype `dskp.ts` | Wire: items are saved one by one (`PATCH /intake/items/{id}`, new rows `POST .../items`, removed rows `reject` with the fixed reason "Dibuang oleh penyemak."); extraction is `POST /intake/batches/{id}/extract` (page button "Ekstrak soalan", polls); the queue is `GET /intake/items`; accept creates a Draf question and never publishes ("Terima (Draf)", tab "Diterima (Draf)", no "Minta Pembetulan"/"Hantar semula"); "Hantar untuk semakan" only runs the batch gate (`GET .../review`) and shows Semakan for the session; the bank reads `GET /questions*` (flat BE-09 shape: no kertas, topic only from `chapterLabel`, version always 1, used-in empty, kind and marks need one detail read per row, at most 50 rows per Bijak subject; Bijak subjects matched to BE-09 subjects by name); bank "Sunting" (`getBankEditable`, `reviseQuestion*`) and `resubmit` stay unavailable | Contract has no version/revision queue, no correction step, no batch submit state, no kertas on a question and no curriculum unit list for the intake editor (it still offers the prototype units; a server unit is shown but a unit picked from the prototype list fails the accept gate "Unit DSKP tiada dalam senarai kurikulum."). (SEJ matches and is what the e2e uses).
